* `DehomogenizedBaseChange.lean`, `ProjectiveCoefficientChartEquiv.lean`:
  coefficient extension of the original reindexed dehomogenized equations
  identifies the actual extended ratio chart with the tensor extension of
  the original ratio coordinate ring.
* `ExtendedGaussRatioCoordinates.lean`: the original tensor chart, in the
  local-model tensor order, and its canonical normalization are identified
  with the actual extended pencil generic point's coordinate rings. The
  chart equivalence is chosen together with its proofs of preservation
  of all original coordinate ratios and extended-field coefficients.
* `ExtendedGaussSourceCoordinates.lean`, `ExtendedGaussSourceNormalization.lean`:
  evaluation at the actual extended ratios agrees with the original tensor
  inclusion on every original source-chart polynomial and hence every
  source-chart function. The normalized coordinate isomorphism preserves
  these same source functions. Corresponding normalized chart primes have
  exactly the same contraction to the original source chart.
* `ReducedClosureChartMorphism.lean`, `ProjectiveSourceChartMorphism.lean`:
  the actual global reduced closed inclusion commutes with its affine
  quotient charts and their original source-ratio coordinate maps.
* `NormalizationCoordinateMorphism.lean`, `ExtendedGaussNormalizationMorphism.lean`:
  the actual global normalization morphism is the specified original
  affine source-ring inclusion in its normalized tensor charts.
* `AffineChartStalkNaturality.lean`, `ExtendedGaussNormalizationStalkMaps.lean`:
  actual global section germs and normalization pullbacks have exactly
  their original affine coordinate values in the actual prime-localized
  stalks, including original source functions represented in pencil sections.
* `ProjectiveCoefficientMorphism.lean`, `ExtendedGaussSourceMorphism.lean`:
  coefficient extension defines the actual projective morphism in the
  original four source coordinates. The extended pencil and its global
  normalization map to that original projective source; its coordinate
  inverse opens are exactly the existing tensor and normalized tensor charts.
* `ProjectiveCoefficientChartMaps.lean`, `ExtendedGaussSourceFractions.lean`:
  the actual global coefficient morphism retains each original projective
  fraction's coordinate denominator and extends its numerator coefficients.
  This agrees with the original source map of the tensor local models.
* `ExtendedGaussProjectiveSections.lean`, `ExtendedGaussPencilSourceDiagram.lean`:
  every actual original projective section has the original source-ratio
  value in the extended tensor chart, and this ring map induces precisely
  the actual global pencil source projection.
* `AffineCoordinateMorphism.lean`, `ExtendedGaussNormalizationSourceDiagram.lean`:
  actual affine section-coordinate isomorphisms recover their actual open
  inclusions. The global normalization chart maps to the pencil tensor
  chart by the normalization ring inclusion, and its global original-source
  projection is the original normalized tensor source-section map.
* `ExtendedGaussProjectiveStalkMaps.lean`:
  the actual geometric original-source stalk map preserves every original
  projective section's source value in the original tensor-normalization
  prime localization. Every original source-chart function has such a section
  representative; its geometric pullback germ equals the original source
  germ of the local normalization model.
* `OriginalProjectiveChartRing.lean`, `AffineSourcePoint.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussSourcePoint.lean`: the original projective chart section
  ring is exactly the original source ratio ring. The actual projective
  source-image prime is its original source contraction under this
  isomorphism, with exactly the same height.
* `ExtendedGaussStalkOrders.lean`, `GlobalNormalizationMultiplicity.lean`,
  `GeometricSourceMultiplicity.lean`: actual global germ order bounds
  become the original prime-localization bounds. The actual projective
  source-image codimension-one condition and orders of actual pulled-back
  homogeneous sections give the original homogeneous common-factor
  divisor power and its degree bound.
* `ExtendedGaussSchemeTensorCharts.lean`, `ExtendedGaussSchemeTensorAtlas.lean`:
  those same original tensor rings and their canonical normalizations are
  the extended scheme's actual affine charts. The normalized charts form
  an explicit four-chart affine cover; their original ring primes have
  the original prime localizations as the corresponding global stalks.
* `NormalizedCurveEtaleOpen.lean`, `ActualGaussEtaleCoverModel.lean`:
  finite normalized affine parameter-curve models, nonzero downstairs
  multiples of nonzero upstairs functions, and construction of a nonzero
  base parameter polynomial defining an etale open for every actual finite
  extension of the closed Gauss field.
* `ResidueTranscendentalParameter.lean`, `PencilDivisorParameter.lean`:
  a transcendental parameter residue makes every nonzero parameter
  polynomial a unit, extending evaluation to the entire rational parameter
  field; the original quotient `p/q` supplies this local parameter and its
  exact source-field and residue-field images.
* `UnramifiedDvrBaseChange.lean`, `DvrBaseChangeRationalOrder.lean`:
  finite coefficient base changes of a source DVR are finite etale
  Dedekind domains when its parameter field is relatively closed in the
  source. Divisors lying above the source DVR exist; their local rings are
  unramified DVRs. A constructed source fraction-field map preserves all
  normalized rational-function orders.
* `HorizontalClosedGaussDivisor.lean`, `ActualPencilDivisorLocalModel.lean`:
  actual closed Gauss field embeddings in horizontal source divisor local
  rings, constructed from the actual quotient `p/q`, and the original
  common-factor degree bound from orders on each finite coefficient base
  change, with normality and unramifiedness derived.
* `IntermediateLocalRing.lean`, `NormalDvrBaseChange.lean`:
  injective intermediate coordinate algebras recover the same source
  divisor local ring; normalization changes neither the horizontal finite
  coefficient base change nor its divisor local rings.
* `SourceLocalNormalization.lean`, `ClosedGaussCoordinateLocalRing.lean`:
  integral coordinate functions embed into normal source local rings,
  whose localizations therefore agree with the contracted localizations
  of the coordinate normalization. The actual closed Gauss coordinate
  algebra and its normalization recover the original source divisor DVR,
  using constructed coordinate maps that preserve all original rational
  functions and polynomials.
* `CoefficientLocalization.lean`, `CoefficientNormalizedLocalRing.lean`,
  `ClosedGaussExtendedLocalRing.lean`: actual coefficient extensions of
  coordinate localization maps, injectivity under flat coefficient
  extension, and contracted-prime local ring identities. For the actual
  closed Gauss coordinate algebra, normalization after finite coefficient
  extension recovers the constructed unramified source divisor DVR.
* `LocalizationFunctionField.lean`,
  `CoefficientNormalizationFunctionField.lean`: equality of the actual
  coordinate and source fraction fields under localization, and explicit
  identification of the canonical extended coordinate normalization with
  the integral closure in the extended source function field.
* `ProjectiveSourceParameterField.lean`, `ProjectiveSourceCoordinates.lean`:
  equal-degree homogeneous quotients lie in the original projective
  source field, which contains the actual closed Gauss field as a
  relatively closed subfield. Actual pencil chart fields equal the
  original-coefficient projective source field. Standard source charts
  have free three-variable polynomial coordinates and the expected
  actual fraction fields and coefficient actions.
* `PrimeLocalizationFunctionField.lean`, `RationalCoverHorizontalLocalRing.lean`:
  prime localization maps into chosen actual source fields, preservation
  of fraction fields and original coefficient actions, and compatible
  local embeddings of rational curve fields from their actual parameters.
* `ProjectiveSourceDivisor.lean`, `ProjectiveHorizontalDivisor.lean`:
  Noetherian actual projective source coordinate rings, principal source
  divisor DVRs with their actual projective fraction fields, horizontal
  closed Gauss field embeddings and finite coefficient Dedekind extensions.
* `AdjoinCoordinateLocalRing.lean`, `ProjectivePencilLocalRing.lean`:
  coefficient adjoin coordinate maps inside source local rings and the
  actual projective pencil chart localization and normalized localization
  identities, preserving every source value and the same fraction field.
* `IntermediateFunctionField.lean`: an injective intermediate coordinate
  algebra inside the original source fraction field has that same field.
* `ValuationLocalDescent.lean`, `ValuationCoefficientLocalRing.lean`,
  `ValuationLocalOverring.lean`: regular source fractions descend through
  dominating local extensions of valuation rings. Compatible coefficient
  fields consequently embed in the original source ring with their same
  source and target values; their rational parameter has transcendental
  residue. A dominating birational local overring is the original source
  valuation ring through an explicit algebra isomorphism.
* `BirationalPrimeLocalRing.lean`, `ProjectivePencilFunctionField.lean`,
  `BirationalNormalizationLocalRing.lean`, `ProjectivePencilContractedPrime.lean`:
  actual pencil chart maps and fraction fields before divisor choice;
  original and normalized coordinate prime local rings over a source divisor
  are the original projective source DVR, without horizontal hypotheses.
* `LocalEquivCoefficientField.lean`, `CoefficientSourceCompatibility.lean`,
  `ProjectivePencilPrimeParameter.lean`, `ProjectivePencilPrimeSource.lean`,
  `ProjectiveContractedPrimeBaseChange.lean`: coefficient-field descent through
  the actual inverse local isomorphism, preservation of source and target
  values, construction of the actual horizontal Gauss parameter, and
  Dedekind finite coefficient extensions derived from prime contraction.
* `LocalEquivCoordinateLocalization.lean`, `IntermediateDenominatorLocalization.lean`,
  `NormalCoordinateLocalization.lean`: recovering coordinate denominator
  localization from a local isomorphism and identifying an already normal
  denominator model as the same localization of coordinate normalization.
* `DenominatorPrimeLocalRing.lean`, `NormalizedPrimeLocalization.lean`,
  `SourceDenominatorPrime.lean`: a given normalization prime over a source
  divisor survives the source denominators and has an explicit local-ring
  isomorphism with its extended prime in the normal source model.
* `SourcePrimeLocalExtension.lean`, `NormalizedPrimeUnramifiedModel.lean`:
  a given normalization prime extends over the source local maximal ideal
  and is identified with an unramified DVR in the finite etale normal model.
* `CoordinateLocalizationFunctionField.lean`, `SourceCoefficientExtension.lean`,
  `CoefficientCanonicalPrimeModel.lean`, `CoefficientNormalizationPrimeContraction.lean`:
  source actions through coefficient extension, the canonical coordinate
  fraction-field realization of the normal source model, and contraction
  and unramified local models for given canonical normalization primes.
* `ProjectivePencilDenominators.lean`, `ProjectiveFinitePrimeModel.lean`,
  `ProjectiveGivenNormalizationPrime.lean`: actual projective source
  denominator models and the canonical finite-extension normalization
  prime local model, with the horizontal parameter and auxiliary original
  prime both derived from the given extended normalization prime.
* `PrimeContractionModelTransfer.lean`, `LocalEquivOrderTransport.lean`:
  transfer of complete local-model conclusions through actual prime
  contraction, preservation of DVR structure and maximal-ideal powers
  under the resulting local isomorphism, and compatible source-order bounds.
* `UnramifiedLocalOrder.lean`, `EtaleDivisorLocalProperties.lean`: exact
  maximal-ideal power comparison in the unramified divisor localization
  and all local source properties of the finite etale divisor model.
* `LocalEquivSourceMap.lean`, `LocalEquivUnramifiedSource.lean`,
  `LocalEquivRationalOrder.lean`: the original source DVR action in the
  normalization local ring, its actual unramified DVR structure, and exact
  normalized valuations on every regular and rational source function.
* `NormalizationLocalSourceOrders.lean`, `CoefficientCanonicalSourceOrders.lean`:
  the complete given-prime regular and rational order comparison in the
  canonical coordinate normalization after finite coefficient extension.
* `ProjectiveFiniteSourceOrders.lean`, `ProjectiveGivenSourceOrders.lean`:
  actual projective normalization prime source-order comparisons, with
  the horizontal parameter constructed from the given extended prime.
* `LocalEquivSourceFieldCompatibility.lean`, `RationalSectionLocalOrder.lean`:
  preservation of original source values, uniqueness of the rational
  pullback, and invariance of rational-section orders under regular local
  frame unit transitions.
* `HomogeneousDehomogenize.lean`, `HomogenizedDivisorLift.lean`:
  fixed-degree chart reconstruction, lifting chart divisors to original
  homogeneous divisors, and preservation of actual prime powers and
  irreducibility under homogenization.
* `PrimitiveDehomogenization.lean`: homogeneous primitive vectors remain
  primitive on their projective charts, so chart common divisibility lies
  in the original homogeneous common factor with its original degree bound.
* `ProjectiveSourceSections.lean`, `ProjectiveChartCommonFactor.lean`:
  actual source chart section coefficients `H_i / X_a^d`, original
  homogeneous chart-divisor equations, and source-DVR section orders
  giving prime-power divisibility of the original common factor.
* `ProjectiveNormalizationMultiplicity.lean`, `PrimeLocalOrderConsequences.lean`,
  `CoefficientProjectiveMultiplicity.lean`, `ActualProjectiveNormalizationMultiplicity.lean`:
  original common-factor divisibility and degree bounds from the given
  canonical finite-coefficient normalization prime's actual section orders,
  with its horizontal data and local order comparison constructed.
* `ProjectiveSourceHeightOne.lean`, `ProjectiveHeightOneMultiplicity.lean`:
  the source divisor equation and DVR are derived from codimension one,
  including when the source prime is the actual normalization prime's contraction.
* `ProjectiveSourceImageDivisor.lean`, `ActualProjectiveImageMultiplicity.lean`:
  the actual affine spectrum image closure, its contracted defining prime,
  and an original homogeneous common-factor bound starting from a dense
  divisor source image with no defining equation supplied.
* `CanonicalSourceLocalMap.lean`, `NormalizationCanonicalOrders.lean`:
  identification of the constructed source-local map with the canonical
  localization map, exact regular orders for that map, and a rational
  extension preserving all normalized divisor valuations.
* `CoefficientCanonicalRegularOrders.lean`: exact canonical source-local
  regular order bounds and a rational pullback preserving every normalized
  source valuation, after finite coefficient extension and canonical
  normalization. The local model and comparison maps are constructed.
* `ProjectiveCanonicalRegularOrders.lean`: the given prime of the actual
  projective finite-coefficient normalization constructs the horizontal
  model and preserves all regular source-local order bounds through the
  canonical localization map, starting from height one on the source.
* `OriginalSourcePrimeRegularOrders.lean`, `GlobalNormalizationRegularOrders.lean`:
  the actual original contracted source prime retains its ideal and
  primality. Its source-divisor order bounds equal the specified original
  source values at the tensor-normalization prime, the corresponding
  actual global source-function germs, and all actual original projective
  chart-section pullback germs. The geometric source-image height-one
  condition supplies the source-divisor hypothesis.
* `AffineCoordinateStalk.lean`, `ExtendedGaussSourceStalk.lean`:
  the actual original projective source stalk is its specified coordinate
  prime localization, with every section germ preserved. The entire actual
  geometric stalk pullback is the canonical source-local map.
* `OriginalSourceCanonicalOrders.lean`, `GeometricSourceStalkOrders.lean`:
  every element of the original source localization, and every locally
  regular function in the actual source stalk, retains its full order
  bounds after actual global pullback. The actual source stalk is a DVR
  when its actual source-image point has codimension one.
* `CanonicalLocalMapCongruence.lean`, `CanonicalRationalOrderComparison.lean`,
  `CoefficientCanonicalRationalOrders.lean`, `ProjectiveCanonicalRationalOrders.lean`,
  `OriginalSourceCanonicalRationalOrders.lean`: exact canonical rational
  pullbacks and all normalized source valuations, constructed for the given
  projective normalized prime and its specified original source map.
* `LocalEquivCanonicalRationalOrders.lean`, `GlobalCanonicalRationalOrders.lean`:
  rational comparisons compose and transport through the actual normalized
  stalk isomorphism. The target is the actual global normalization DVR;
  its source-local map agrees with the entire geometric stalk pullback.
* `RationalStalkPullbackComparison.lean`, `FramedRationalStalkComparison.lean`,
  `GeometricRationalSourceOrders.lean`, `GeometricRationalSectionOrders.lean`:
  the rational map extends all actual source-stalk values and is uniquely
  characterized by them. Every normalized source valuation agrees with
  the actual global normalization valuation, in every compatible regular
  local frame, under the actual source-image codimension-one condition.
* `AffineStalkDimension.lean`, `GlobalSourceCodimension.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussPointAtlas.lean`, `GlobalPointRegularOrders.lean`:
  affine prime height equals the dimension of the actual stalk. Every
  global normalized point has an original tensor-prime representative,
  so intrinsic source codimension one gives all regular stalk orders
  without a chart prime among the caller's hypotheses.
* `RationalComparisonFractionField.lean`, `IntrinsicDivisorPullback.lean`,
  `GlobalPointDivisorOrders.lean`: fraction-field coordinate changes preserve
  normalized valuations. The entire actual source stalk map has the
  regular, rational and compatible-frame comparison in the fraction field
  of the actual source stalk, at every point over a source divisor.
* `GlobalDivisorImagePrime.lean`, `GlobalCurveDivisorOrders.lean`:
  density of an arbitrary prime closed subvariety's actual source image
  in a divisor constructs its original affine prime with height-one source
  contraction and gives the intrinsic order comparison at its generic point.
* `PolynomialProjectiveProperties.lean`, `SchemeDivisorPullback.lean`,
  `GlobalSchemeDivisorOrders.lean`: integrality and local Noetherianity of
  the original projective source, actual scheme function-field pullbacks,
  uniqueness from the entire source stalk, and equality of Mathlib's
  integer orders of vanishing, including every regular local-frame unit.
* `ReducedClosureLift.lean`, `ExtendedGaussPencilOriginalMap.lean`:
  reduced-image factorization through a point closure constructs the
  actual extended-pencil morphism to the original closed pencil. It
  retains the original generic point and inverse coordinate opens.
* `ProjectiveCoefficientEvaluation.lean`, `ExtendedGaussPencilCoefficientSections.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussPencilOriginalChartMap.lean`: compatibility on coefficients
  and ratios determines the actual projective section pullback. The full
  original Gauss coefficient field and all original-pencil chart sections
  have their canonical left tensor-inclusion values.
* `ProjectiveCoefficientBaseMap.lean`, `ProjectiveChartBaseMap.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussPencilChartBaseMaps.lean`: actual projective coefficient
  morphisms commute with their specified coefficient bases, and both
  pencil-chart structure maps are the canonical tensor coefficient maps.
* `ClosedGaussPencilOriginalAtlas.lean`, `SchemeBaseChangeCharts.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussPencilCartesianCharts.lean`, `ExtendedGaussPencilBaseChangeComparison.lean`,
  `ExtendedGaussPencilBaseChange.lean`: original affine charts, actual
  inverse-open Cartesian squares, gluing of the categorical base-change
  property, and an actual global isomorphism with both projections retained.
* `ProjectiveCoefficientComposition.lean`, `ClosedGaussPencilBaseChangeSource.lean`:
  coefficient projections compose, so the base-change isomorphism retains
  the actual original projective source morphism. The constructed global
  normalization has a finite map to that categorical base change, preserving
  both projections and its entire original source morphism.
* `Laplacian.lean`: the complete polynomial computation of §5.4.
* `Shear.lean`: actual polynomial algebra automorphisms and their inverses.
* `StableShear.lean`: equation (6.11), with the paper's composition convention.
* `Tame.lean`: the tame subgroup, elementary automorphisms and composition identities.
* `TameShearFactors.lean`, `TameShear.lean`: the elementary factors and actual
  five-variable tame group membership.
* `ShearJacobian.lean`: the full four-variable Jacobian and its cube-zero identity.
* `ShearSubstitution.lean`, `ShearAutomorphism.lean`: the four-variable shear
  and its explicit inverse, over the original field.
* `Proposition64.lean`: the polynomial-ring stabilization identification and
  assembly of the general assertion of Proposition 6.4.

All module paths above are beneath `DimensionFourDependence/`.
* `DvrNormOrderBound.lean`, `PolynomialNormOrderBound.lean`: relative ideal
  norms bound an arbitrary normalization element's local order by its base
  norm order, and then by its norm polynomial's degree.
* `RationalInfinityDegree.lean`, `RationalNormDegree.lean`: the actual rational
  infinity valuation and the norm of a section regular in both frames give
  a nonzero polynomial norm of degree at most the actual field degree.
* `PolynomialNormalizationSectionBound.lean`: the resulting arbitrary-section
  order bound on the actual polynomial-base integral closure.
* `NormalLocalSectionBound.lean`, `NormalLocalHypersurfaceSectionBound.lean`:
  construct the normalization prime and local isomorphism from the given
  normal local ring, preserve the arbitrary section's exact value, and use
  the original hypersurface equation to construct the field-degree bound.
  Finite-chart and infinity-frame integrality remain hypotheses here.
* `InfinityScaledMonicRoot.lean`: bounded total coefficient degrees construct
  a monic integral equation for the scaled fiber coordinate over the actual
  rational-function infinity valuation ring.
* `MonicProjectionSection.lean`, `MonicProjectionSectionBound.lean`: actual
  normalized finite and infinity chart coefficients with their hyperplane
  transition construct both integrality conditions and the arbitrary-section
  multiplicity bound, without assuming section integrality or field degree.
* `MonicHypersurfaceProjection.lean`, `HypersurfaceProjectionSectionBound.lean`:
  the original full-fiber-degree equation constructs the monic equation,
  all infinity coefficient bounds and the original total-degree section bound.
* `LinearMonicProjectionChoice.lean`, `LinearProjectionCoordinateChange.lean`,
  `ConstructedMonicProjectionCoordinates.lean`: the original nonzero equation
  chooses the actual invertible linear projection, retaining the exact
  original total degree, actual affine generators and all their equations.
* `MonicAffineFiniteProjection.lean`, `ConstructedAffineFiniteProjection.lean`:
  construct module finiteness of the entire original affine chart over the
  actual algebra generated by the remaining projection coordinates.
* `TensorPencilFiniteProjection.lean`: the original tensor atlas coordinates
  and extended pencil equation construct a finite linear projection on every
  actual tensor chart, with the unchanged pencil-degree bound.

* `ValuativeIntegralCriterion.lean`, `ValuationProjectiveLinearFrame.lean`:
  actual function-field valuation rings detect integrality; every nonzero
  linear frame with regular coordinate ratios supplies an original unit
  coordinate and its original projective chart.
* `ProjectiveNormalizedSection.lean`: actual regular coefficients on the
  four original normalized coordinate charts, their hyperplane transition,
  and the resulting section integrality in original-field linear frames.
* `ScaledProjectionCoordinateIntegrality.lean`,
  `RationalProjectionCoordinateIntegrality.lean`: exact original matrix
  reconstruction, actual homogeneous linear frames and the integral finite
  and infinity projection coordinate values.
* `ProjectiveSectionProjectionRegularity.lean`,
  `OriginalChartProjectiveSectionBound.lean`: the original normalized
  chart coefficients construct both projection regularities and the
  arbitrary-section local degree bound, given the actual projection values.
* `ProjectionGeneratorOriginalField.lean`: original affine generators
  construct changed field generation and the single projection fiber
  generator, without an auxiliary generation hypothesis.
* `FiniteProjectionIndependence.lean`, `FiniteProjectionResidueParameter.lean`,
  `CanonicalProjectionResidueParameter.lean`, `TensorPencilProjectionResidue.lean`:
  finite actual surface projections have independent base coordinates;
  every actual divisor prime on the original tensor-pencil normalization
  constructs a transcendental residue among those same base coordinates.

* `IndependentRationalProjection.lean`, `IndependentProjectionField.lean`:
  independence constructs transcendence over the actual rational parameter
  field and the full iterated rational projection embedding, with its
  actual variables, polynomial values and original constants.
* `ResidueIndependentProjectionField.lean`, `ResidueProjectionPolynomialActions.lean`,
  `RationalInfinityTargetAction.lean`, `ResidueProjectionActionCompatibility.lean`:
  construct all actual local, polynomial, rational and infinity scalar
  actions from the original residue parameter and independent coordinate,
  and verify their original values and compatibility.
* `FiniteProjectionLocalCenter.lean`, `OriginalChartNonfieldSectionBound.lean`:
  a finite projection at a nonfield local ring constructs the irreducible
  base polynomial needed for the original-chart section bound.
* `NormalizationPrimeFunctionField.lean`, `CanonicalProjectionFieldActions.lean`:
  the canonical normalization prime has the original fraction field and
  retains every original affine coordinate and constant; its actual finite
  surface projection constructs the entire iterated rational field action.
* `TensorPencilProjectionFieldActions.lean`: every actual original tensor
  pencil divisor constructs those compatible projection actions internally.
* `OriginalChartResidueProjectionBound.lean`, `CanonicalOriginalChartSectionBound.lean`:
  the arbitrary original-chart section degree bound from actual independent
  residue projection coordinates, without supplied scalar actions, a base
  polynomial or an auxiliary normal local model.

* `ReindexedProjectiveSectionRegularity.lean`, `ReindexedProjectionFieldGenerator.lean`,
  `ReindexedOriginalChartSectionBound.lean`, `ReindexedResidueProjectionBound.lean`:
  the actual selected projection order preserves field generation, both
  original-frame section regularities and the hypersurface degree bound.
* `SelectedProjectionCoordinates.lean`, `CanonicalFiniteProjectionSectionBound.lean`:
  the actual divisor chooses its independent residue parameter, and base
  variable reindexing retains the original equation and its exact degree.
* `ConstructedOriginalSectionBound.lean`: the original affine surface
  generators and nonzero equation construct the entire arbitrary
  original-frame section degree bound without supplied projection data.
* `ProjectiveAffineRatioCoordinates.lean`, `TensorPencilOriginalSectionMultiplicity.lean`:
  the original actual tensor-pencil ratios and pencil equation give the
  arbitrary original-normalized-frame section bound at every divisor prime.
* `OriginalChartSectionCoefficient.lean`, `OriginalChartCanonicalSectionOrder.lean`:
  the coefficient itself constructs its actual original affine normalization
  value and local section value, retaining its exact rational function.
* `TensorPencilCanonicalSectionMultiplicity.lean`: the original pencil degree
  bounds that canonical coefficient, without a supplied local value.
* `OriginalChartSectionStalkTransport.lean`, `GlobalOriginalSectionMultiplicity.lean`:
  canonical original-frame coefficients supply their actual global
  normalization germs, retain all maximal-ideal power orders and satisfy
  the original pencil-degree bound at intrinsic codimension-one points.

* `NormalDivisorStalkDvr.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizationDivisorDvr.lean`:
  normality, Noetherianity and intrinsic codimension one construct a DVR
  at every actual normalization divisor, without a source-image assumption.
* `RegularSectionIntegerMultiplicity.lean`, `OriginalSectionGermNonzero.lean`,
  `RegularFrameIntegerOrder.lean`, `GlobalHyperplaneIntegerMultiplicity.lean`:
  nonzero glued section coefficients have nonzero actual germs, bounded
  nonnegative integer multiplicities in the actual scheme function field,
  and unchanged integer orders under every regular local frame unit.
* `NormalizedHyperplaneTransitions.lean`, `NormalizedHyperplaneCocycle.lean`:
  both original normalized charts restrict to their actual common overlap;
  coordinate ratios give regular units satisfying the actual triple-overlap
  cocycle, with all original rational values retained.
* `NormalizedHyperplaneGluing.lean`: actual normalized-overlap gluing of
  hyperplane coefficients is equivalent to the coefficient representation
  used by the original-pencil section bound, preserving all coefficients.
* `ExtendedNormalizedChartSections.lean`: actual normalized chart
  structure-sheaf functions give their canonical original normalization
  coefficients and injective integral fraction-field values.

* `SchemeRationalSectionFunctions.lean`, `RationalFrameLocalPredicate.lean`:
  actual regular functions act by restriction-compatible rational germ
  values on every open, and local framed regularity is a proved local predicate.
* `RationalFrameSectionSubmodule.lean`, `RationalFrameModuleSheaf.lean`:
  local regularity gives actual structure-sheaf modules, semilinear
  restrictions, and a genuine scheme module sheaf with its sheaf axiom proved.
* `SchemeLocallyRegularRationalFunctions.lean`, `RationalFrameLocalCoefficient.lean`:
  local regular representatives glue to a unique actual regular function,
  constructing the coefficient of a section in a nonzero original frame.
* `RationalFrameRegularCoefficient.lean`, `RationalFrameLocalTrivialization.lean`,
  `RationalFrameLineBundle.lean`: actual regular transition ratios construct
  rank-one linear trivializations on every subopen, natural under restriction;
  a frame cover proves the actual module sheaf locally free of rank one.
* `RationalFrameGlobalSections.lean`, `RationalFrameGlobalGluing.lean`:
  actual global sheaf sections construct a faithful common rational numerator
  and unique actual regular chart coefficients, with nonvanishing and
  actual regular-function overlap gluing derived from the section itself.

* `AffineChartFunctionFieldEquiv.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizationFunctionField.lean`:
  original normalized chart fraction fields identify with the actual scheme
  function field, preserving actual regular-section generic and local germs.
* `ProjectiveCoordinateRatioSections.lean`, `ProjectivePullbackRationalCoordinates.lean`:
  original coordinate ratios define actual regular projective sections;
  geometric pullback retains their ratio identities in the own scheme function field.
* `ProjectivePullbackHyperplaneSheaf.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizationHyperplaneSheaf.lean`:
  the original pencil morphism constructs its nonzero rational frame atlas,
  actual regular transitions, and a genuine locally free rank-one module sheaf.
* `ExtendedNormalizationHyperplaneSections.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizationHyperplaneGluing.lean`:
  actual global pencil-sheaf sections supply original integral chart coefficients,
  nonzero for nonzero sections, with exact common rational numerator and
  actual regular-unit overlap gluing.

* `AffineChartSectionNaturality.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizedChartPullbacks.lean`:
  actual geometric section pullbacks equal their affine coordinate identifications;
  normalization retains every pencil-chart regular function's original tensor value
  in the canonical normalization, original fraction field and own scheme function field.
* `ProjectiveCoordinateRatioEvaluation.lean`: actual regular coordinate sections
  are canonical homogeneous-localization sections and evaluate to the original
  ratio-chart algebra generators.

* `ExtendedPencilAmbientPullbacks.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizedAmbientValues.lean`:
  actual ambient section pullbacks have their original tensor values; the
  original tensor coordinates equal the native hyperplane-frame weight ratios.
* `ProjectiveConstantSections.lean`, `ExtendedNormalizedCoefficientValues.lean`:

* `ProjectiveChartThreeGenerators.lean`, `ExtendedOwnCoordinateCharts.lean`:
  the entire original tensor chart is its native original-frame ratio algebra;
  every actual regular normalized-chart function is integral over this algebra.
* `ExtendedNativeNormalizedSections.lean`, `ProjectiveNormalizedSectionFieldMap.lean`,
  `ExtendedOwnFunctionFieldEquiv.lean`, `ExtendedOriginalSectionRepresentation.lean`:

* `ExtendedNormalizedChartGerms.lean`, `ExtendedNativeSectionGerms.lean`:

* `ExtendedNativeSectionMultiplicity.lean`: arbitrary nonzero actual native

* `NormalSchemeRationalRoots.lean`, `RationalFrameRootExtension.lean`:

* `RationalFramePowers.lean`, `RationalFramePowerFrames.lean`:

* `ProjectiveHomogeneousPullbackCoefficients.lean`, `ProjectiveHomogeneousPullbackSections.lean`:
  original homogeneous source forms have actual regular chart-polynomial

* `ExtendedHomogeneousPowerSections.lean`, `ExtendedHomogeneousSectionCoefficients.lean`:
  original source forms, map components and ruling-linear output forms give actual

* `ExtendedRationalRootSections.lean`, `ExtendedRationalRootMultiplicity.lean`:

* `SchemeIntegerOrderArithmetic.lean`, `RationalFrameGlobalValueArithmetic.lean`:
  actual integer orders respect nonzero powers and product-power identities;
  actual global section addition and regular scalar multiplication retain their values.
* `ExtendedFrameIntegerOrders.lean`:
  actual original regular transition units preserve all powered-frame orders;
  the native hyperplane-section bound holds in every original frame at a divisor.
* `ExtendedNativeLinearCombinations.lean`:
  extended constants are actual global regular functions, original input linear

* `ExtendedProductPowerMultiplicity.lean`:
  the original product-power identity, actual regularity and vanishing of U

* `ExtendedSourceFormLocalIdeals.lean`, `ExtendedOutputSpanMultiplicity.lean`:

* `ProjectiveChartPolynomialSections.lean`, `ProjectiveChartPolynomialCoefficientChange.lean`:
  actual regular ratio-polynomial sections equal the original source standard-frame

* `ExtendedNativeSourceFormPullbacks.lean`:
  native source-form germs are exactly the actual original-source stalk pullbacks;
  their local ideal orders supply the geometric orders required by Lemma 4.2.
* `ReducedHomogeneousPencilDegree.lean`:
  the reduced denominator divides every alternative denominator; every given

* `NativePencilCommonFactorContradiction.lean`, `NativePencilSourceDivisorExclusion.lean`:
  native component orders give an original homogeneous common-factor power;

* `NativePencilSpreadFamily.lean`, `RulingRootProductIdentity.lean`:

* `ExtendedOriginalHomogeneousRelations.lean`, `ExtendedParameterOutputRelations.lean`:
  homogeneous relations over the original closed Gauss parameter field persist
  through the actual tensor coordinate charts to the native field; original
  output-covector relations give actual native component-incidence equations.
* `RulingFormCoordinateSpan.lean`, `RulingFormRelations.lean`:
  independent forms on a two-dimensional ruling construct all coordinate
  coefficients; kernel factorizations preserve the coordinate and binary-form

* `ExtendedActualGaussRuling.lean`, `ExtendedChosenGaussRuling.lean`:
  the original nonplanar map constructs an actual two-dimensional extended

* `NativeIndependentRulingExclusion.lean`:
  independent m,n and the relation s=alpha m+beta n with alpha nonzero on that

* `PolynomialCovectorCoefficientKernel.lean`, `SmallKernelScalarExtension.lean`:
  a finite original coefficient matrix exactly describes polynomial plane
  incidence. At a parameter transcendental over the point field, incidence
  gives every coefficient equation. A kernel of dimension at most one stays
  an original constant line after any coefficient-field extension.
* `NormalPlaneConstantIntersection.lean`, `PolynomialPlaneParameterRigidity.lean`:
  independent normal jets bound the entire constant plane intersection by one
  vector dimension. Independent-parameter incidence therefore makes every
  projective point ratio an original constant; a nonconstant point forces
  the parameter algebraic over its point field.
* `MovingPlanePointFieldDimension.lean`, `ActualGaussPlaneFixedCurve.lean`:
  the original map constructs its primitive polynomial Gauss plane family
  with this fixed-curve obstruction. If a two-dimensional family field is
  algebraic over the full point coordinate field and the plane parameter,
  incidence forces the source point field to have dimension two. The criterion

  construction below now supplies one from the actual nonzero root/product data.

* `RationalPlanePolynomialSpecialization.lean`, `ActualGaussPlaneSpecialization.lean`,

  polynomial representative and coefficient-kernel bound and forces the original
  source image to be an irreducible hypersurface.
* `ExtendedRootCombinationVanishing.lean`, `ExtendedInputLinearCoefficient.lean`,
  `ExtendedInputLinearResidue.lean`: vanishing of the actual root combination
  forces both root and tangent-plane section to vanish. The actual regular chart

  the original residue plane equation. No divisor order estimate is needed here.
* `ActualRootVanishingSourceDivisor.lean`, `ActualGaussIndependentRootExclusion.lean`:

* `PrincipalVanishingDivisor.lean`, `ExtendedNonunitSectionDivisor.lean`:

* `ActualGaussRootUnitCoefficients.lean`: the original independent ruling-root
  equations derive nonvanishing of U and force each original chart coefficient

* `RadialIntegralUnitObstruction.lean`: a nonzero radial linear value has no
  polynomial-integral inverse. An integral cone element with that actual value
  is a nonunit and, in a Noetherian domain, has a height-one zero prime.
* `ProjectiveConeCoordinateAlgebra.lean`, `ProjectiveConeFractionConstants.lean`:
  the original point defines an actual finite-type radial cone, which evaluates

* `ExtendedOwnCoordinateFieldGeneration.lean`, `ExtendedNativeCone.lean`:
  the actual native affine coordinates generate the whole actual surface field.
  The original map constructs its own finite-type radial cone of dimension at

* `ClosedGaussCoefficientRealization.lean`: the entire relatively closed

* `ParametricGraphHessian.lean`: the rational parameter chain rule and Euler
  identity derive the ruling equation, nonzero denominator, parameter

* `SymmetrizedCubicFactor.lean`, `ParametricGraphCubic.lean`: symmetric tensor

* `ModifiedCubicFiberBoundary.lean`: the original cubic plus a linear multiple

* `RulingDirectionAcceleration.lean`: a parameter-algebraic ruling direction
  fixes its own coordinates. The modified boundary equation then gives
  the second directional derivative of n c equal to zero.
* `ParametricGraphBaseChange.lean`: the denominator and cubic factor retain
  their entire values after coefficient extension. The quotient Hessian's

* `ActualDevelopableGraphEquations.lean`: the original homogeneous map derives
  the actual ruling, nonzero denominator, Hessian and cubic factor, and the
  actual output Euler weight 1+1/d of its first-invariant product n c.
* `ActualDevelopableBoundaryEquation.lean`: the original actual quadratic and
  cubic fiber equations derive the modified boundary equation and ruling

* `ActualFiberBoundaryExistence.lean`: the original nonzero vertical tangent

  direction and a point at infinity in the actual projective fiber closure.
* `CurveValuationResidueAlgebraic.lean`: a nontrivial valuation of a field of
  relative dimension at most one has residue field algebraic over its

* `ActualFiberBoundaryResidue.lean`: the original map supplies the actual
  curve dimension; its nonzero boundary direction has algebraic residue and

* `ActualDevelopableBoundaryWitness.lean`: the actual projective boundary
  point, residue algebraicity, finite coordinate extension, ruling and

* `ActualDevelopableBoundaryConstruction.lean`: the original homogeneous

* `DerivationSurfaceConstants.lean`: on a surface field, a normalized

* `WeightedAffineRootFactorization.lean`: zero second directional derivative
  and Euler weight 1+1/d construct a binary linear factor times a dth root
  of a transverse linear coordinate, including the zero-slope case.
* `AlgebraicBinaryRootFactorization.lean`: the actual uniquely extended binary

  All constructed coefficients belong to one actual finite extension.

* `ProjectiveConeRootLift.lean`, `ExtendedNativeConeRoots.lean`: actual
  homogeneous polynomial values and their positive-power roots lift to the

* `NormalizationDivisorNonvertex.lean`, `ExtendedNativeConeNormalization.lean`:
  a height-one cone normalization prime avoids an original coordinate. The

* `UnitSectionCoefficientInverseIntegral.lean`, `NormalizationConeChartObstruction.lean`:
  a unit original projective coefficient forces its inverse coordinate ratio

* `ExtendedNativeConeZeroTransfer.lean`: a nonvertex cone zero forces a nonunit

* `IndependentGaussRootConfigurationExclusion.lean`: the constructed divisor

* `ActualRootDivisorSpreadConstruction.lean`: the closure of this actual zero
  point spreads out to an integral family dense in its original source divisor.

* `IntegralCoordinateFieldGeneration.lean`, `NormalizationResidueCoordinateField.lean`:
  normalization-prime quotient integrality and algebraic generation of its
  canonical fraction field from the original coefficient and coordinate values.
* `RationalParameterCoefficientAlgebraicity.lean`, `ClosedGaussCoefficientImages.lean`:
  a field containing the actual rational Gauss parameter makes every actual
  extended closed-cover coefficient algebraic.
* `TensorPencilResidueFieldGeneration.lean`: at every original tensor
  normalization prime, the actual residue fraction field is algebraic over
  its actual source point field with the actual Gauss parameter adjoined.
* `NormalizationResidueDimension.lean`, `TensorPencilResidueDimension.lean`:
  a surface normalization divisor has positive relative residue transcendence
  degree; actual tensor pencil divisors have total transcendence degree at least two.
* `AffineGeneratedPointCoordinates.lean`, `AffinePointFieldDimension.lean`,
  `TensorPencilResiduePointDimension.lean`: the original affine values construct
  their own projective point and generate its entire point field. Polynomial
  moving-plane incidence forces the actual divisor source point field to have
  the full residue dimension, at least two, without supplied field generation
  or residue dimension hypotheses. The conditional geometric source-image bridge is checked below.

* `NormalizationResidueDimensionExact.lean`, `TensorPencilResidueDimensionExact.lean`:
  proper normalization prime quotients strictly lower finite transcendence
  degree. Actual tensor divisor residues have relative dimension one and
  total dimension two; moving-plane incidence gives source point field
  dimension exactly two.
* `PrimeQuotientCodimensionOne.lean`, `GeneratedPointPrimeDimension.lean`:
  a two-dimensional prime quotient of a factorial affine threefold has
  height one. The actual coordinate relation quotient has the dimension
  of its generated point field.
* `TensorPencilResidueSourcePrime.lean`: evaluation through the actual
  original source map agrees with the actual residue coordinate values,
  so their relation prime equals the original source contraction in the
  source chart's original polynomial coordinates, preserving height.
* `TensorPencilMovingPlaneSourceDivisor.lean`: the actual divisor source
  contraction has height one under the polynomial moving-plane incidence
  criterion. Its actual affine source-image closure is an irreducible
  hypersurface, and its actual global projective source-image point has
  codimension one. Actual Gauss-plane incidence and the source image are now
  derived for every zero divisor. The actual radial-cone construction now
  supplies such a divisor from the nonzero root/product data.

`DimensionFourDependence.lean` imports the checked project.

## Outstanding proof obligations

Theorem 1.1 and Proposition 2.1 are complete over every original
characteristic-zero field in every positive homogeneous degree. The native
homogeneous ternary dependence theorem, both second-orbit cases, all
four-variable nilpotent ranks and constant-relation descent are proved.
The full paper remains incomplete for the following reasons.

Some geometric formulations in Section 4 remain open. These include
the passage from the abstract line-bundle hypotheses of Lemma 4.4 to the
specified original divisor, its intersection-theoretic formulation, and
the categorical hyperplane line-bundle and ampleness identifications.
The checked dependence proof uses the algebraic results recorded above.
`validation/audit.json` gives the scope of each result.

No theorem declaration with an admitted proof has been inserted.
The neighboring quartic project's final theorem is not imported: that theorem
still depends on an admitted surface argument. Six independent foundation
sources or fragments were adapted, and all were recompiled in this project.
Their exact source hashes and the scope of each adaptation are recorded in
`validation/foundation_provenance.json`.

The milestone notes below retain the intermediate scopes at the time of
those steps. The current status at the top of this file and the current
audit flags supersede older statements of open gaps.

## Reproduce the checks

Toolchain: Lean 4.34.0 and Mathlib v4.34.0.

For a standard Lean installation:

```text
lake update
lake build
lake env lean validation/axioms.lean
```

On this workstation, the existing Mathlib cache can be reused read-only:

```text
python scripts/check.py --timeout 600
python scripts/audit.py
```

Both scripts accept `--lean-bin` and `--packages` to override the local paths.
The longer compilation allowance covers the global normalization-stalk proofs.
`scripts/check.ps1` is an alternative PowerShell compilation driver.
The cache directory contains only Mathlib and its dependencies on `LEAN_PATH`;
the neighboring project's theorem library is excluded.

The audit recompiles a file containing `#print axioms` for every exported
definition, structure and theorem. It accepts only Lean's standard
`propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`, checks that the compiled modules
are current, matches each current source hash against a successful compilation,
and records SHA-256 hashes of the sources and paper. It explicitly records
both main theorems as incomplete, the general assertion of Proposition 6.4
as complete, and its final low-degree ordinary-tameness assertion as incomplete.
It also records the checked field theory, all three fiber invariants, actual
function field, affine and projective component assertions, Hilbert polynomial,
and positive integral Hilbert-degree bound over the actual coefficient field
of Lemma 2.2. It also records invariance under field extension, graph-hyperplane
degree preservation, compatibility with the full output field, and coefficient
constancy on every geometric component. Lemma 2.2 and its full-output,
full-source-space geometric generic degree conclusion are checked. Both main
theorems and the outstanding classifications and geometric arguments remain
explicitly incomplete.

The audit also records construction of the image normal, the actual
height-one image prime and defining hypersurface, its irreducible projective
closure, and the linear-equation/dependence equivalence. The algebraic
constant-plane criterion and iterated tangent-pairing identities are checked.
The geometric fixed-plane assertion of Proposition 2.3 is complete, including
exact constants, varying tangents, geometric nonlinearity comparison, and
descent of the plane translation. Its direction-image-curve assertion and
the later developable-image exclusion are explicitly incomplete.

Section 3.1 is complete. The audit separately records the first-order Taylor
identity, construction of the maximal common power, primitive fixed-image
exclusion, actual radial projective point, specialization of fiber equations,
and radial exclusion on every actual geometric generic component. Section 3.2
and Proposition 3.1 are now checked. Sections 4–5 and both main theorems remain incomplete.
The audit also records the constructed Euler weights and radial derivative
identities at the start of Section 3.2 and the scalar-extended isotropic
quadratic coordinate expansion. The normalized direction-to-line step is
checked, including algebraic partial extension, rank-two orthogonality,
zero acceleration, the exact line relation and transport to the actual image
equation. The quadratic isotropic directions are constructed from the actual
rank-two coefficient Hessian over algebraically closed extensions. Their
normalization, rank preservation, compatible algebraic coefficient extension
and integration to two actual generic image lines are now assembled. The
Gauss-image normal-ratio function field is identified with its actual
affine chart coordinate-ring fraction field, and its transcendence degree
equals the original coefficient Hessian rank. The doubly ruled quadric
classification and actual defining-equation nonsingularity are now checked,
including the arbitrary-field assembly of Proposition 3.1. The
elementary three-skew-lines step is now checked separately: disjoint
two-dimensional subspaces in dimension four supply the complementary
subspaces and their standard coordinates. Their determinant polynomial
has nonzero gradient at every nonzero vector. Quadratic forms through
the three lines are unique up to scalar; their common transversals form
the standard opposite ruling and cover that quadric. The existence and
function-field dimension of actual covering line components are now checked
separately below. The choice of three actual skew members is also checked
below; the needed dense incidence between the actual families is now checked as well.
The pairwise-intersecting-family plane-or-point alternative is now checked,
as is selection of three skew members on any nonempty open chart of an
abstract preirreducible family with open skewness. The actual image's line
charts are now constructed from finite coefficient equations obtained by
substitution into the actual hypersurface equation. Every projective line
admits a coordinate-pivot chart, and the resulting coordinate-permuted
atlas covers all lines on the hypersurface over an infinite field. Skewness
is an explicit nonvanishing determinant condition and is open on every
chart and induced subfamily; incidence with a fixed chart line is closed.
The topology is identified with one induced from the polynomial prime
spectrum, so the line charts are Noetherian and have finitely many
irreducible components. The two actual generic image lines now give two
distinct atlas points representing distinct projective spans. Their
original-field parameter equations are checked under arbitrary field
extension, and each point lies over an original-field minimal-prime chart
component. The quotient parameter ring with two line variables constructs
that component's universal incidence family and its specialization to the
actual generic image point. Its kernel is exactly the actual image ideal,
so its spectrum image has closure equal to the actual affine image cone.
The component and universal incidence spectra are irreducible. Each such
covering component now also has parameter function-field dimension one.
Gluing the full atlas remains separate; the incidence argument works
directly with the original-field covering chart components.
The finiteness step at smooth base-field points is now checked as well.
Contained lines lie in the actual three-dimensional tangent kernel. An
irreducible nonlinear homogeneous hypersurface contains no projective plane,
proved using a rank-three plane projection and its height-one relation prime.
The nonzero plane restriction yields a nonzero slope polynomial, hence only
finitely many contained lines through the point. The actual image equation
has the needed nonlinearity from component independence. Injectivity of
each matrix chart then gives finite chart-parameter fibers at every smooth
projective base-field point. The actual image coordinate quotient embeds
in the source polynomial ring, and tensoring this embedding with any field
extension remains injective. This identifies the extended image ideal
exactly and proves it prime; the actual image equation remains irreducible.
The actual generic output point stays nonzero and has nonzero evaluated
gradient, so its lines and original-field chart-parameter fibers are finite
over every extension of its actual output field.
The generic matrix and two line coordinates generate the universal incidence
field. The output coordinates already contain those two line coordinates,
so the matrix entries generate the incidence field over the output field.
Every embedding fixing the output sends the matrix into the checked finite
fiber. Finitely many embedding-conjugate matrices force the extension to
be algebraic. The exact original image relations identify its output field
with the original image function field of transcendence degree three.
The actual incidence field consequently has transcendence degree three;
subtracting the two free line coordinates gives parameter function-field
dimension one. This is proved for every covering component supplied by an
actual generic image line, with no assumed dimension or fiber comparison.
Over algebraically closed coefficients, the actual matrices satisfying
each component prime now have exactly that vanishing ideal by the
Nullstellensatz. Their evaluation-prime image has the same vanishing ideal,
so they form a nonempty irreducible family in the actual chart topology.
Their quotient evaluations separate the parameter ring; evaluation in the
two free line coordinates then separates the universal incidence ring.
A common plane therefore supplies a nonzero original component relation.
A common vertex supplies the original polynomial tangent-normal relation,
and Gauss dimension two excludes it. Thus each actual covering component
is neither coplanar nor concurrent. Coordinate permutation preserves
skewness and its openness; the irreducible-family argument now selects
three actual pairwise skew member lines in every nonempty open subfamily.
Every nonempty open subfamily still has dense actual output image:
containment of a line in any fixed polynomial equation is closed in the
matrix parameters, and irreducibility extends its vanishing from the open
part to the entire component. Separation and the exact incidence kernel
then identify the output vanishing ideal with the original image ideal.
Finally, if two actual covering components are pairwise incident, these
results construct a skew triple from the first and place all lines of the
second on its determinant quadric. The determinant is an actual nonzero
homogeneous quadratic relation; the irreducible image equation is a
nonzero scalar multiple and has degree two. The image's zero set is
exactly that determinant quadric in invertible linear coordinates.
The incidence relation between the two actual covering components is
now derived. The actual product of their matrix points has a prime
vanishing ideal, equal to the radical of the separate original coefficient
ideals. Both original coordinate rings embed into its quotient, and their
images generate it. The generated-algebra dimension bound gives dimension
at most two for this product of covering curves. A proper prime
specialization strictly lowers finite transcendence degree.
For the lower bound, the four original linear point equations of a line
pair give an actual incidence matrix in every two chosen charts. Its
kernel is exactly the represented line intersection. Two distinct lines
through the nonzero generic output have a rank-three incidence matrix.
Rank is preserved under field extension, and its one-dimensional kernel
descends to the pair's generated parameter field. Thus their projective
intersection vector is defined over that field up to one scalar. The
actual output field has dimension three, so the parameter-pair field has
dimension at least two. The specialization therefore preserves dimension
and is injective: the actual pair has exactly the product's relations.
The actual original-field incidence determinant consequently vanishes on
every member pair. The preceding skew-triple and covering-density argument
now gives the quadric without an incidence or dimension hypothesis.
For every constructed fiber witness, the actual last-output coefficient
and the three invariant coefficients are also constructed in that
witness's coefficient field. This assembles the two-line construction
and quadric identification from the original map and actual nonlinear
full-output geometric generic component. The original defining equation
has degree two and an exact nonzero scalar-multiple determinant identity
in actual invertible linear coordinates. The checked gradient chain rule
shows that its evaluated gradient is nonzero at every nonzero vector.
This proves the smooth-quadric conclusion of Proposition 3.1 over
algebraically closed coefficients. The arbitrary-coefficient-field assembly
is now checked as well. The original actual generic point has exactly the
extended image relations: its evaluation prime is minimal over the integral
coefficient extension, and the extended image ideal is already prime. The
actual Jacobian rank, component independence, and independence of the normal
polynomials are preserved. An algebraic closure of the original coefficients
embeds in the actual geometric fiber's constants; the two original actual
lines then give the geometric smooth quadric without rebuilding that fiber
or assuming a Gauss-dimension comparison under field extension. Projective
gradient nonvanishing also descends to every original-field point. The actual
Gauss-image dimension bound follows separately from the nonzero radial
Hessian kernel vector. The main theorems remain open.
Zero Gauss dimension now also forces a plane over the original coefficient
field, using coordinate derivations and homogeneous polynomial proportionality
without algebraic closedness. Thus a nonplanar original rank-three image
has Gauss dimension exactly one or two. With an actual nonlinear geometric
generic component, these reduce to the developable case or the geometrically
smooth quadric case. The respective exclusions in Sections 4 and 5 remain open.
The projective image's actual function-field dimension two is now checked
over the original coefficient field as well. The affine output field is
generated by its projective ratios and a single nonzero output. Euler weight
annihilates the actual ratio field but gives that output a nonzero derivative,
so it is transcendental over the ratios. Rank three consequently leaves
projective dimension two, identified with the actual chart-coordinate prime
quotient's fraction field. Applying the same dimension difference to the
actual positive-degree normal identifies Gauss dimension plus one with its
polynomial Jacobian rank. This rank and normal commute with coefficient
extension; the linear-equation case is zero over both fields. Thus the actual
Gauss-image dimension is preserved under arbitrary coefficient extensions.
For Section 4.1, the rational-section incidence argument is now checked
algebraically. A primitive source-linear form is irreducible, and the actual
parameter/source exchange preserves this property. Gauss's lemma transfers
it to the original source fraction field; a rational root forces parameter
degree at most one. Under a primitive rational parameter presentation of
the actual image normal, zero radial pairing therefore bounds the actual
normal component span by two and yields original-field component dependence.
The parameter presentation itself is now constructed. Positive actual Gauss dimension gives a coordinate
not constantly proportional to the pivot, two actual original-field source
points with different ratio values, and their actual joining line with a
transcendental restricted ratio. Localization away from the actual pivot
transfers every original ratio relation to the source line. The actual Gauss
coordinate quotient has dimension one, and a new kernel would strictly lower
that dimension, contradicting the transcendental restricted coordinate.
Thus the entire actual Gauss function field embeds in the line's field with
all normal-ratio coordinates preserved. The proved Lüroth theorem gives an
actual rational-function-field equivalence and transcendental generator over
the original coefficients. Clearing the finitely many denominators and
extracting their polynomial gcd constructs the primitive polynomial covector
and nonzero actual scalar normal presentation. The preceding rational-section
argument now proves the full actual zero-radial-pairing exclusion, including
the zero-dimensional Gauss case. Section 4.1 is complete; Proposition 4.1
and the later developable-image and smooth-quadric exclusions remain open.
The actual first fiber invariant is now nonzero in every independent output
